Let's detail natural Adam.

Functions:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0: return uniform
    return e/s
```
Need avoid underflow: clip z to [-700,700]? But if n large, exp(-700) zero. Center. Use `z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)`? This restricts min p ~1.9e-22*n? Actually exp(-50) ~1.9e-22, for n=1000 min 1e-25, okay. But flexibility may be limited; could set +-100 for n small, 80 for large. Use range based on n: L= min(80, max(30, np.log(n*1e6)))? For epsilon floor 1e-20, L=46. Need p min maybe 1e-20. But optimizer may need zeros. For safe, L=50. For n=4096, p min 1.9e-22/4096 ~4e-26, positive. Good. Use z clipped to [-50,50]. But L-BFGS bounds maybe [-50,50].

`_smooth_value_and_weights(c, beta)`:
- m = max(c)
- val = m + logsumexp(beta(c-m))/beta
- w = exp(beta(c-m))/sum. If beta maybe 0? If beta<=1, uniform? For β high. Use if exp overflow.
Return val,w,m.

Gradient for z:
```
def _natural_grad_z(z, beta):

    # adapt beta: beta *=1.02? or if stuck increase
    # Adam

    # line search

    for trial in range(10):

       pt=softmax(zt); ct=conv; vt,wt=lse(ct,beta)

       step *=0.5

       else: fail +=1
       z=zt
       update m,m2 using grad (old or new? Use old grad)
       compute exact score; update best
    else:
       # no line search accept
       fail +=1
       # perturb / reduce beta / reset z

    # if stuck, increase beta or restart from best
```

L-BFGS method:

```
_LBFGS_STATE = {'beta': 1.0, 'n': 1, 'n_calls':0, 'deadline':0}
def _lbfgs_fun(x):
    # x is z, can be any length in STATE
    zs = x - np.mean(x)
    p = softmax(clip(zs))
    c = conv(p)
    w,val = lse(c, beta)
    fbar = dot(w,c)
    H = corr(w,p)
    grad = 2*p*(H-fbar)
    grad = grad - grad.mean()
    # add tiny mean penalty? Since we subtract mean in fun/grad, flat direction removed. Add 1e-8*zs to grad? maybe.
    grad = grad + 1e-8*zs
    return val + 1e-8*0.5*np.sum(zs*zs), grad
```

Global state for L-BFGS:

_LBFGS = {'beta':1.0, 'lambda':1e-8, 'n_calls':0}
def _lbfgs_fgrad(x):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    z = x - np.mean(x)
    z = np.clip(z, -80,80)
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    p = _softmax(z)
    c = _fft_self(p)
    W,val = _lse_weight_value(c, _LBFGS['beta'])
    H = _correlation(W,p)
    f = float(np.dot(W,c))
    grad = 2.0*p*(H-f)
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    pen = 0.5*1e-8*float(np.dot(z,z))
    g = grad + 1e-8*z
    return val + pen, g
```
Scipy minimize expects fun returning scalar or (f,grad) if jac=True. Good.
Potential issue: `_fft_self` returns nonnegative? If p from softmax, fine.

    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=mean; z=clip
    state['beta']=beta
    bounds=[(-80.0,80.0) for _ in range(n)]
    try:
       res = minimize(_lbfgs_fgrad, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':max_iter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
    except Exception: pass
    z=res.x if ... else z
    a=_softmax(z); return normalize(a), score
```

SLSQP function:
```
def _qp_fun(x):
    a=x[:n]; lam=x[n]
    r = M @ a - lam
    f = 0.5 * dot(r,r)
    g = np.empty_like(x); g[:n]= M @ r; g[n]= -float(r.sum())
    return f,g
```
But helper cannot access M via closure. Could use global state again. Or use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with top-level callback that reads global `_QP_STATE` containing M,n. That's fine.

```
def _spectral_init(n, rng, iters=8):
    L=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N < 2*L: N*=2 # or L? DFT length >=L. Use next pow2 L maybe enough? For linear convolution zero pad N>=2n-1=L. Use N next pow2 L.
    flat = np.ones(L, dtype=float) # target c shape. Normalize? Target sum1 => flat/L.
    T = np.fft.fft(flat, N).astype(complex)
    # We want A^2 ≈ T. Choose A0 = sqrt(T + eps), with stable branch.
    eps = 1e-12 * max(1, abs(T).max())
    A = np.sqrt(T + eps)
    # Make A[0] positive real
    if A[0].real <0: A = -A
    a = np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(iters):
       a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
       a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s <= 1e-300:
           a = _make_ushape(n,1.0)
       else: a/=s
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       # enforce desired magnitude/phase?
       mag = np.abs(A); ph=np.angle(A)
       des_mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(T)+eps)
       des_ph = 0.5*np.angle(T)
       # blend phase toward desired: comp = current unit * exp(i*0.5*(des_ph - ph))? Equivalent weighted geometric mean.
       factor_ph = np.exp(0.5j*(des_ph - ph))
       A = (A / (mag + eps) * des_mag) * factor_ph
       A[0] = abs(A[0]) # positive
    return a
```

Let's code `_spectral_init` robust:
```
def _spectral_init(n, rng, box_iters=10, Nfactor=4):
    L=2*n-1
    N=1; minN = max(4*n, L+1); while N<minN: N*=2
    target = np.zeros(N); target[:L]=1.0/L
    T = np.fft.fft(target)
    eps = 1e-10 * max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(T))))
    des = np.sqrt(T + eps) # principal; T complex from real target, near zeros.
    # choose sign so sum a maybe positive: flip if a sum negative? After inverse direct.
    A = des.copy()
    best_a = None; best_score=1e100
    for _ in range(box_iters+1):
       a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
       a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s>1e-300:
          a/=s
          sc = _score_fft(a)
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       Ap = np.fft.fft(a, N)
       # Complex geometric blend toward des
       # protect zeros: ratio = des/(Ap+eps)
       ratio = des / (Ap + 1e-12 + 0j)
       # limit ratio magnitude
       rmag = np.abs(ratio)
       rmag = np.clip(rmag, 0.1, 10.0)
       ratio = rmag * np.exp(1j*np.angle(ratio))
       A = 0.9*Ap + 0.1*des? Maybe A = Ap * ratio**gamma? Use `A = Ap * np.power(ratio, 0.3)` but complex power branch.
       # Simpler: A = Ap * (0.7 + 0.3*ratio)
       A *= (0.7 + 0.3*ratio)
       # enforce A[0] positive real: A[0]=abs(A[0])
    return best_a or uniform
```

Let's draft code structure.

```
"""
... docstring
"""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
try:
    from scipy.optimize import lsq_linear as _scipy_lsq_linear  # maybe not use
except Exception:
    _scipy_lsq_linear = None

`_qp_fun`:
```
def _qp_fun(x):
    M = _QP_STATE['M']
    n = _QP_STATE['n']
    a = x[:n]
    lam = x[n]
    r = M.dot(a) - lam
    f = 0.5 * float(np.dot(r,r))
    g = np.empty(n+1)
    g[:n] = M.dot(r)  # since M symmetric; if M not exactly symmetric, use M.T.dot(r), but M symmetric. Use M.T.dot(r) for correctness.
    g[n] = -float(np.sum(r))
    return f, g
```
Because M2 symmetric? W index i+j, yes symmetric. Use `M.T.dot(r)` to be safe. M is dense matrix; `M.T` view.

Now natural optimizer implementation details. Let's write robust but not too long.

```
def _optimize_natural(a, deadline, rng=None, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, lr_init=0.1, beta_factor_start=5, beta_factor_end=80):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8: return a, _score_fft(a)
    L = int(np.log(max(n,2)) * 4)?? Use 60.
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z = _center_clip(z, 70.0)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = _score_fft(a); best_z = z.copy()
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    t = 0; lr = lr_init; fail=0; it=0; last_imp=0
    K = beta_factor_start
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c = _fft_self(a)
       maxc = float(np.max(c))
       beta = K / max(maxc, 1e-12)
       W, val = _lse_w_val(c, beta)
       H = _corr(W, a)
       fbar = float(np.dot(W, c))
       grad = 2.0 * a * (H - fbar)
       grad -= np.mean(grad)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)) or np.max(np.abs(grad)) < 1e-15:
           # perturb
           ...
       t +=1
       mm = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
       vv = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
       mm *= 1/(1-beta1**t); vv *= 1/(1-beta2**t)
       step = lr * mm/(np.sqrt(vv)+1e-8)
       # line search
       accepted=False
       z_try = z
       max_try = maxc
       val_try = val
       step_try = step
       for _ in range(8):
          zt = z - step_try
          zt = _center_clip(zt, 70.0)
          vt, mt = _z_value(zt, beta)
          if vt <= val + 1e-12 or mt < maxc - 1e-12:
              accepted=True; z=z_try=zt; val=val_try=vt; maxc=max_try=mt; break
          step_try *=0.5
          if np.max(np.abs(step_try)) < 1e-8: break
       if accepted:
           if val_try < val + 1e-12? already
           # update momentum after accept? We already updated with old grad. Could leave.
           if max_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc = max_try; best_a=_softmax(zt); best_z=zt.copy(); last_imp=it
           # if val improved, lr up
           if vt < val_old - 1e-10: lr = min(0.3, lr*1.05); fail=0
           else: fail += 1
       else:
           fail += 5
           lr *=0.5
           if fail > 80:
               # restart from best with noise or temper
               if rng.random()<0.5:
                   z = best_z + rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)
               else:
                   z = z * 0.7
               z = _center_clip(z,70.0)
               m[:]=0; v[:]=0; t=0; lr=lr_init; K=max(beta_factor_start, K*0.5); fail=0
       # periodic adaptive K
       if it % 20 ==0:
          K = min(beta_factor_end, K*1.05 + 0.02)
       if it - last_imp > 200 and it>50:
          # perturb from best
          z = best_z + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
          z = _center_clip(z,70.0)
          m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=lr_init; K=max(beta_factor_start, K*0.6); fail=0
       it +=1
    # final exact? use score_fft
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Need track `val_old` for improvement; define before line search. In line search, if no acceptance but some trial had mt<best_sc? We could update best if candidate exact score even if not accepted? However if val worsened but maxc improved, interesting. Let's do: inside loop, if mt < best_sc - 1e-12: best_a = _softmax(zt); best_z=zt; best_sc=mt. Even if later not accepted. This allows exploring.
For acceptance criterion, maybe accept if val worsened slightly but exact max improved: `if mt < maxc - 1e-12 or vt <= val_old - 1e-12`. Good.

def _optimize_lbfgs(a, beta_factor, deadline, maxiter=50):
   if _scipy_minimize is None: return a, score

   if finite: z=res.x else x0
   a2=normalize(softmax(z)); sc=_score_fft(a2); return a2 if sc<best? else a
```
But `_lbfgs_fun` uses `_LBFGS_STATE` beta. However objective is constant under z shift; we add penalty. Need include penalty in both fun/grad:
```
def _lbfgs_fun(x):
    beta = _LBFGS_STATE['beta']
    z = x - np.mean(x)
    z = np.clip(z, -70,70)
    p = _softmax(z)
    c = _fft_self(p)
    W,val = _lse_w_val(c,beta)
    H = _corr(W,p)
    f = float(np.dot(W,c))
    grad = 2*p*(H-f)
    grad -= np.mean(grad)
    pen = 0.5*1e-8*float(np.dot(z,z))
    g = grad + 1e-8*z
    return val + pen, g
```
L-BFGS-B may be slow if bounds list creation for n large. Limit to n<=2048. For 2048, bounds list okay but L-BFGS many iterations maybe heavy. Use maxiter 30.
Could call L-BFGS multiple times with increased beta. But time.

```
def _solve_eq_for_W(W, a0, n, maxiter):
   L = 2*n-1
   rows = np.arange(n)[:,None]
   cols = np.arange(n)[None,:]
   M = L * W[rows + cols]
   _QP_STATE['M']=M; _QP_STATE['n']=n
   lam0 = float(np.mean(M.dot(a0)))
   x0 = np.empty(n+1); x0[:n]=a0; x0[n]=lam0
   bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] for...; lam_bound=10*L
   cons = {'type':'eq','fun':_qp_eq,'jac':_qp_eq_jac}
   res = _scipy_minimize(_qp_fun, x0, jac=True, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-8})
   x = res.x if ...
   return _normalize(x[:n])
```

```
def _optimize_ls(a, deadline, rng, max_n=1024, K_start=4, K_end=50, mix_start=0.5):
    a = _normalize(a); n=a.size; best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fft(a)
    if n==0 or _scipy_minimize is None: return best_a,best_sc
    c=_fft_self(a); maxc=max(c); L=2*n-1; uniformW=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    K=K_start; mix=mix_start; fail=0; last=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_fft_self(a); maxc=...; sc=2*n*maxc
       if sc < best_sc: best...
       beta=K/max(maxc,1e-12)
       Wc,_=_lse_w_val(c,beta)
       W = (1-mix)*uniformW + mix*Wc
       if n > max_n: # fallback natural one step? maybe return
       newa = _solve_eq_for_W(W,a,...)
       if newa is None: newa=a
       # line search
       best_t = 0.0; best_mix_sc = sc
       for t in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05):
           cand = (1-t)*a + t*newa; cand=_normalize(cand); s=_score_fft(cand)
           if s < best_mix_sc:
               best_mix_sc=s; best_t=t; best_mix=cand.copy()
       if best_t >0 and best_mix_sc < sc - 1e-12:
           a = best_mix; fail=0
           if best_mix_sc < best_sc: best_sc=...; last=it
       else:
           # if small t improves slightly? no
           fail += 1
           if fail > 3:
               # mix a bit with newa if it doesn't worsen too much
               cand = 0.8*a + 0.2*newa; cand=normalize; s=score
               if s <= sc + 0.002:
                   a=cand; fail=0
               else:
                   a=_normalize(a + 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)); fail=0
       # adapt
       K = min(K_end, K*1.02 +0.05)
       mix = min(1.0, mix + 0.01)
       if it - last > 15:
           K=max(K_start, K*0.5); mix=0.5; fail=0
       it +=1
    return best_a,best_sc
```
If newa is same as a, line search no improve. SLSQP may be expensive. Limit maxiter:
`maxiter = 60 if n <= 256 else 30 if n<=512 else 15`.

But SLSQP with W one-hot at high K may produce trivial: equalize a row for one bin -> minimize? It may produce zeros; line search sees. Good.

Implement `_param_inits` without nested.
```
def _param_inits(n, rng, count=20):
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    d=np.abs(2*x-1)
    qs=[0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]
    amps=[0.3,0.6,0.9]
    ms=[1,2,3,5]
    idx=0
    for q in qs:
      for amp in amps:
        m=ms[idx % len(ms)]
        phase=rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        a=0.12+0.88*(d**q)
        a=a+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+phase)
        a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a=_normalize(a)
        out.append(a)
        idx+=1
        if idx>=count: break
      if idx>=count: break
    # add a few pure U/no cos
    for q in [0.8,1.6,2.5]: out.append(_normalize(0.1+0.9*(d**q)))
    # smooth random
    for _ in range(4):
       a=_make_smooth_random(n,rng,_make_uniform(n), amp=0.6)
       out.append(a)
    return out
```

`_make_smooth_random` previous: lowpass random multiplicative noise. Implement top-level.
```
def _make_smooth_random(n,rng,base,amplitude=0.5):
   x=base.copy()
   z=rng.normal(size=n)
   F=np.fft.rfft(z)
   cutoff=max(2,min(len(F), n//12+2))
   if cutoff<len(F): F[cutoff:]=0
   f=np.fft.irfft(F,n).real
   f=f-np.mean(f); sd=f.std(); if sd>0: f=f/sd
   a=x*(1+amplitude*f)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-10)
   return _normalize(a)
```

`_spectral_init` maybe returns one. Implement robust and not call `_score_fft` inside? It can. Use rng for sign? We'll include a couple of variants (direct des, -des, phase random). Maybe heavy but okay.
```
def _spectral_init(n, rng, iters=6):
    L=2*n-1
    N=1; minN=max(4*n, L+1)
    while N<minN: N*=2
    target=np.zeros(N); target[:L]=1.0/L
    T=np.fft.fft(target)
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(T))); eps=1e-10*max(1.0,scale)
    des=np.sqrt(T+eps)
    best=None; best_sc=1e100
    for sign in [1,-1]:
       A=sign*des.copy()
       for it in range(iters):
          a=np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
          a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum()
          if s>1e-300:
             a/=s; sc=_score_fft(a)
             if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
          Ap=np.fft.fft(a,N)
          # blend
          ratio=des/(Ap+1e-12)
          mag=np.abs(ratio); mag=np.clip(mag,0.2,5.0); ratio=mag*np.exp(1j*np.angle(ratio))
          A = Ap * (0.7 + 0.3*ratio)
          A[0]=abs(A[0]) # real positive
       final=...
    if best is None: return _make_ushape(n,1.0)
    return _normalize(best)
```
Mix branch: if sign=-1, A=-des; but after `A[0]=abs(A[0])`, sign lost. Instead first direct `a0=ifft(des).real`, clip; `a1=ifft(-des).real`, clip. For blend, keep sign. In blend, A[0]=abs? If sign negative, making positive may flip sequence to positive? Not sure. We can do direct variants.
Maybe simpler: return best of direct `ifft(des)` and `ifft(-des)` and one blend. Good.

def _optimize_natural(a, deadline, rng=None, beta_factor_start=8.0, beta_factor_end=120.0):
    a = _normalize(a); n=a.size
    Lbound=70.0
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=_center_clip(z,Lbound)
    best_sc=_score_fft(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_z=z.copy()
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(12347)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=0.15; K=beta_factor_start; fail=0; it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_fft_self(a); maxc=float(np.max(c)); sc=2*n*maxc
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       beta=K/max(maxc,1e-12)
       W,val=_lse_w_val(c,beta)
       H=_corr(W,a)
       fbar=float(np.dot(W,c))
       grad=2*a*(H-fbar); grad-=np.mean(grad)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
          ...
       maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if maxg < 1e-15:
          # perturb
          z = best_z + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); z=_center_clip; m/v reset; continue
       t+=1
       mm=0.9*m+0.1*grad; vv=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
       mhat=mm/(1-0.9**t); vhat=vv/(1-0.999**t)
       step=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       maxstep=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
       accepted=False
       step_try=step
       val_old=val; max_old=maxc
       for _ in range(10):
          zt=z-step_try; zt=_center_clip(zt,Lbound)
          p=_softmax(zt); cp=_fft_self(p); vtp,mtp=_lse_w_val(cp,beta)
          mt=float(np.max(cp))
          if mtp? Actually mtp from lse is val not maxc? `_lse_w_val` returns val, not maxc. Need separate maxc: `mt = float(np.max(cp))`. Accept if vtp <= val_old - 1e-12 or mt < max_old - 1e-12.
          if mt < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=mt; best_a=p.copy(); best_z=zt.copy(); last=it

          if accept: z=zt; maxc=mt; val=vtp; accepted=True; break
          step_try *= 0.5
          if float(np.max(np.abs(step_try))) < 1e-8: break
       if accepted:
          if val < val_old - 1e-12: lr=min(0.5, lr*1.08); fail=0

          fail += 3
          lr *= 0.6

```
def _optimize_phase(a, deadline, rng, use_lsq=True, use_lbfgs=True, lsq_frac=0.4, nat_frac=0.45, lbfgs_frac=0.15):
   start=time.monotonic(); total=max(1,deadline-start)
   if use_lsq and _scipy_minimize is not None and a.size<=1024 and total>30:
      end_ls=min(deadline-20, start+total*lsq_frac)
      a,s=_optimize_ls(a,end_ls,rng,max_n=1024)
   if time()<deadline-20:
      end_nat=min(deadline-10, start+total*(lsq_frac+nat_frac))
      a,s=_optimize_natural(a,end_nat,rng)
   if use_lbfgs and a.size<=2048 and time()<deadline-5:
      maxiter = 60 if a.size<=256 else 30 if a.size<=1024 else 15
      a2,s2=_optimize_lbfgs(a, 30, maxiter=maxiter)
      if s2<s: a,s=a2,s2
   return a,s
```

```
def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng=None, K=15):
   a=normalize; n=a.size; best_a=a; best_sc=score_fft; best_c? 
   it=0; fail=0
   while time < deadline:
      c=conv; maxc; sc
      if sc<best...
      beta=K/max(maxc,1e-12)
      W,_=lse(c,beta)
      H=corr(W,a); h=H-np.mean(H)
      hmax=max(abs(h)+1e)
      a_mean=a.mean()
      best_local=a; best_local_sc=sc
      # multiplicative
      for frac in (0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.2):
         eta = frac / hmax
         cand = a * np.exp(-eta*h)
         cand=np.maximum(cand,0); sc=score_fft(cand)
         if sc < best_local_sc: best_local=cand; best_local_sc=sc
      # euclidean
      for frac in (0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3):
         alpha = frac * a_mean / hmax
         cand = a - alpha*h; cand=np.maximum(cand,0); sc; update
      if best_local_sc < sc - 1e-12:
         a=best_local; fail=0
      else:
         fail+=1
         if rng is None: rng=default
         if fail>5:
            # mix with noise
            a=_normalize(a + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n)); fail=0
      if it % 10==0: K=min(50,K*1.05)
      it+=1
      if not finite break
   return best_a,best_sc
```
This is essentially projected subgradient with line search for hard max. It can refine final exact score. Use before final exact.

This may be more directly improving score than smooth. Let's include as final polish for all lengths up to 4096 (FFT cheap). It uses FFT score, but exact at end. It can also improve 256.

def _optimize_stage(a, deadline, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=max(1, deadline-t0)
    if allow_lsq and n<=512 and _scipy_minimize is not None and total>30:
       frac = 0.35 if n<=256 else 0.25
       end = min(deadline-30, t0+total*frac)
       a,s=_optimize_ls(a,end,rng)
    # natural
    if time()<deadline-10:
       if n<=256: frac_nat=0.35
       elif n<=512: frac_nat=0.4
       elif n<=1024: frac_nat=0.5
       else: frac_nat=0.6
       end = min(deadline-20, t0+total*(frac_so_far+frac_nat))
       # easier: end = min(deadline-20, time.monotonic() + max(10, total*frac_nat))
       a,s=_optimize_natural(a,end,rng)
    # L-BFGS with fixed maxiter if allow and n<=1024 and time
    if allow_lbfgs and n<=1024 and time()<deadline-15:
       maxiter = 80 if n<=256 else 40
       a2,s2=_optimize_lbfgs(a, 25, maxiter=maxiter)
       if s2<s: a=s...
    # subgrad
    if time()<deadline-5:
       a,s=_subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng)
    return a,s
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```
Returning size 1 zeros if empty? But evaluator rejects empty; we won't. If a size 0, uniform 1? Better return array length 1 [1]. But if input empty, n=1, score2. Could.
```
if a.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
```

`_next_pow2`: as before.

`_fft_self`:
```
def _fft_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(1)
    L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    b=b.real
    if b.size < L: b=np.pad(b,(0,L-b.size)) # not needed
    b=b[:L]
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    return b
```

`_score_fft`:
```
def _score_fft(a):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    c=_fft_self(a); m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return 2.0*float(n)*m
```

`_exact_score_seq`:
```
def _exact_score_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.nan_to_num(a); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    if n <= 0: ...
    # if n > 20000 use fft
    if n <= 8192:
       b=np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
    else:
       b=_fft_self(a)
    m=float(np.max(b))
    return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
Since internal normalized sum1, s=1. But final maybe.

`_lse_w_val`:
```
def _lse_w_val(c,beta):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size==0: return np.ones(1),0.0
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        return np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size), m
    arg=beta*(c-m)
    arg=np.clip(arg, -745.0, 709.0)
    e=np.exp(arg)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
       return np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size), m
    w=e/s
    val=m + float(np.log(s))/beta
    return w,val
```

`_corr`:
```
def _corr(W,a):
    W=np.asarray(W,float).ravel(); a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if W.size != 2*n-1:
       # resize/pad? In case.
       tmp=np.zeros(2*n-1); L=min(tmp.size,W.size); tmp[:L]=W[:L]; W=tmp
    N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ar=a[::-1]
    Ar=np.fft.rfft(ar,N)
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(W,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Ar*Wr,N).real
    H=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    H=np.where(H>0.0,H,0.0)
    return H
```
If n=1, 3n-2=1, N=1, slice [0:1]. Good.

`_center_clip`:
```
def _center_clip(z,L=70.0):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return z
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -L, L)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z
```

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.where(z < -745.0, -745.0, z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return e/s
```

def _z_value(z,beta):
    p=_softmax(z); c=_fft_self(p); w,val=_lse_w_val(c,beta); mc=float(np.max(c)); return p,c,w,val,mc
```

`_param_inits`: ensure no lambdas.
```
def _param_inits(n,rng,count=24):
    n=max(int(n),2)
    out=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    d=np.abs(2.0*x-1.0)
    specs=[(q,amp,m) for ...]? Avoid list? Can nested loops.
```
Nested loops okay, no closures.
```
    qs=(0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0)
    amps=(0.3,0.6,0.9)
    ms=(1,2,3,5)
    k=0
    for q in qs:
      for amp in amps:
         if k>=count: break
         m=ms[k % len(ms)]
         ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
         a=0.12 + 0.88*(d**q)
         a = a + amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*m*x + ph)
         a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
         out.append(_normalize(a))
         k +=1
      if k>=count: break
    for q in (0.9,1.8,2.8):
       a=0.1+0.9*(d**q); out.append(_normalize(a))
    for _ in range(3):
       base=_make_uniform(n)
       out.append(_make_smooth_random(n,rng,base,0.6))
    return out
```
`_make_uniform` define:
```
def _make_uniform(n): n=max(int(n),1); return np.ones(n)/n
```

`_make_smooth_random` as above.

`_spectral_init`: Use rng but not much. Need avoid complex issues. Write:
```
def _spectral_init(n, rng, iters=5):
    n=max(int(n),2)
    L=2*n-1
    N=1; minN=max(4*n, L+1)
    while N < minN: N <<= 1
    target = np.zeros(N)
    target[:L] = 1.0 / float(L)
    T = np.fft.fft(target)
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(T)))
    eps = 1e-10 * max(1.0, amax)
    best = None; best_sc = np.inf
    seeds = (1.0, -1.0)
    for sign in seeds:
        A = sign * np.sqrt(T + eps)
        # Make A[0] real positive to align sum
        if A[0].imag != 0 or A[0].real < 0:
            # do not simply discard? If flip, sign maybe
            if A[0].real < 0 or abs(A[0].imag) > abs(A[0].real):
                A = np.conj(A)?
        # Direct inverse
        for it in range(iters):
            a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                a /= s
                sc = _score_fft(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc; best = a.copy()
            Ap = np.fft.fft(a, N)
            # Blend frequency toward desired
            ratio = np.sqrt(T + eps) / (Ap + 1e-12 + 0.0j)
            mag = np.abs(ratio)
            mag = np.clip(mag, 0.2, 5.0)
            ratio = mag * np.exp(1j * np.angle(ratio))
            A = Ap * (0.7 + 0.3 * ratio)
            # Keep zero frequency positive real
            A[0] = float(np.abs(A[0]))
    if best is None:
       best = _normalize(0.1 + 0.9 * np.abs(2*np.linspace(0,1,n)-1))
    return _normalize(best)
```
The `if A[0].imag != 0` no. Simpler: direct A = sign*sqrt; then if A[0].real<0: A=-A maybe sign not used. Actually if sign=-1, making A[0] positive flips sign; so both signs same after. But iters with blend to `des` positive zero freq loses sign. Instead for sign=-1, use desired `-des` in ratio? We can pass `des = sign*sqrt(T+eps)`. Then target zero positive? If sign=-1, des[0] negative. But sum a? Desired A[0] negative would make mean negative; not good. We can have two des: positive and maybe phase shifted. Direct des and des with phase π for all frequencies? `-des` is just inverse negative, clipping from negative may yield complement? Could be different. For blend, use `des` with zero freq positive maybe. Use direct sign without zero correction? Hmm.
Simpler: only positive des; direct and one phase random? The init not crucial. Let's not overcomplicate:
```
des = np.sqrt(T + eps)
A = des.copy()
for it...
# also direct inverse of des and -des
a1 = ifft(des).real; a2=ifft(-des).real; evaluate
```
In iteration, enforce A[0]=abs(A[0]) to positive. Good. Direct a2 captured via candidate. Good enough.

```
def _resample_sequence(old,new_n):
   old=np.asarray(old,float).ravel(); old=nan_to_num; max0=sum normalize
   new_n=max(int(new_n),2)
   if old.size==0: return uniform
   if old.size==new_n: return old
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
   return _normalize(new)
```
If old size 1, np.interp works. Good.

   return arr/s

QP functions:
```
def _qp_eq(x):
    n = _QP_STATE['n']
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 1.0)
def _qp_eq_jac(x):
    n = _QP_STATE['n']
    jac = np.empty(n+1, dtype=float)
    jac[:n]=1.0; jac[n]=0.0
    return jac
def _qp_fun(x):
    M = _QP_STATE['M']; n=_QP_STATE['n']
    a = x[:n]
    lam = float(x[n])
    r = M.dot(a) - lam
    f = 0.5 * float(np.dot(r,r))
    g = np.empty(n+1,float)
    g[:n] = M.T.dot(r)
    g[n] = -float(np.sum(r))
    return f, g
```
`M.T.dot(r)` for symmetric. `M` could be None? Not called unless set.

`_solve_qp_target(a,W, maxiter)`:
```
def _solve_qp_target(a, W, maxiter):
    if _scipy_minimize is None: return a
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    # build M
    rows = np.arange(n)[:, None]
    cols = np.arange(n)[None, :]
    idx = rows + cols
    M = float(L) * W[idx]
    # ensure symmetry? M = 0.5*(M+M.T) to correct. Efficient.
    M = 0.5*(M + M.T)
    _QP_STATE['M']=M; _QP_STATE['n']=n
    a = _normalize(a)
    lam0 = float(np.mean(M.dot(a)))
    x0 = np.empty(n+1)
    x0[:n]=a; x0[n]=lam0
    lam_bound = max(10.0, 10.0*float(L))
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n + [(-lam_bound, lam_bound)]
    cons = {'type': 'eq', 'fun': _qp_eq, 'jac': _qp_eq_jac}
    try:
       res = _scipy_minimize(_qp_fun, x0, jac=True, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-8, 'disp': False})
    except Exception:
       _QP_STATE['M']=None; return a
    x = getattr(res,'x', None)
    if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       _QP_STATE['M']=None; return a
    na = np.asarray(x[:n],float)
    na = np.maximum(na,0.0)
    s=na.sum()
    if s <=1e-300:
       _QP_STATE['M']=None; return a
    na /= s
    _QP_STATE['M']=None
    return na
```
SLSQP may modify x0? no. Setting state M=None after frees.
Memory: `idx` huge; after M built, idx freed? It remains local until function end; M holds. Could `del idx, rows, cols, W` to free before SLSQP. But W local reference. Add del.

Potential SLSQP with `constraints` as dict not list; accepted. Yes scipy accepts single constraint dict. Good.

```
def _optimize_natural(a, deadline, rng=None, beta_factor_start=8.0, beta_factor_end=150.0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline: return a,_score_fft(a)
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(1234)
    Lb = 70.0 if n <= 2048 else 60.0
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = _score_fft(a); best_z = z.copy()
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.15
    K = beta_factor_start
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c = _fft_self(a)
       maxc = float(np.max(c))
       sc = 2.0 * n * maxc
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
           best_sc = sc; best_a = a.copy(); best_z = _center_clip(np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)), Lb)
       beta = K / max(maxc, 1e-12)
       W, val = _lse_w_val(c, beta)
       H = _corr(W, a)
       fbar = float(np.dot(W, c))
       grad = 2.0 * a * (H - fbar)
       grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
           z = best_z + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); z=_center_clip(z,Lb); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; lr=0.15; K=max(beta_factor_start, K*0.5); fail=0; it+=1; continue
       maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if maxg <= 1e-16:
           z = best_z + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n); z=_center_clip(z,Lb); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; lr=0.15; fail=0; it+=1; continue
       t += 1
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad
       v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(grad*grad)
       mhat = m / (1.0 - 0.9**t)
       vhat = v / (1.0 - 0.999**t)
       step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
       val_old = val
       max_old = maxc
       accepted = False
       step_try = step
       for _ls in range(10):
           zt = z - step_try
           zt = _center_clip(zt, Lb)
           pt = _softmax(zt)
           cpt = _fft_self(pt)
           mt = float(np.max(cpt))
           _, vtp = _lse_w_val(cpt, beta)
           if mt < best_sc - 1e-15:
               best_sc = mt; best_a = pt.copy(); best_z = zt.copy(); last = it
           if vtp < val_old - 1e-13 or mt < max_old - 1e-13:
               z = zt
               a = pt
               val = vtp
               maxc = mt
               accepted = True
               break
           step_try = step_try * 0.5
           if float(np.max(np.abs(step_try))) < 1e-9:
               break
       if accepted:
           if val < val_old - 1e-13:
               lr = min(0.5, lr * 1.1)
           else:
               fail += 1
       else:
           fail += 4
           lr *= 0.6
           if fail > 80:
               z = best_z + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
               z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
               m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; lr=0.15; K=max(beta_factor_start, K*0.5); fail=0
       if it - last > 250 and it > 50:
           z = best_z + 0.04*rng.standard_normal(n)
           z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
           m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; lr=0.15; K=max(beta_factor_start, K*0.7); fail=0
       if it % 8 == 0:
           K = min(beta_factor_end, K * 1.04 + 0.02)
       it += 1
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Potential issue: bias correction denominator `1-0.999**t` for t large okay, for t=0 not used. If t resets to0 then okay. However `0.9**t` for t large underflows to0, denom1. Good.
If `lr` becomes too small, perturb uses reset lr=0.15.

def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng=None, K_start=8.0, K_end=60.0):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n<2 or time.monotonic()>=deadline: return a,_score_fft(a)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(888)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fft(a)
    K=K_start; fail=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_fft_self(a); maxc=float(np.max(c)); sc=2.0*n*maxc
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       beta=K/max(maxc,1e-12)
       W,_=_lse_w_val(c,beta)
       H=_corr(W,a)
       h=H - float(np.mean(H))
       hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-300:
           # perturb
          a = _normalize(a + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n)); fail+=1; it+=1; continue
       a_mean=float(np.mean(a))
       local_a=a; local_sc=sc
       # multiplicative
       for frac in (0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.2):
           eta = frac / hmax
           cand = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
           sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
           if sc_c < local_sc - 1e-15:
               local_sc=sc_c; local_a=cand.copy()
       # euclidean
       for frac in (0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3):
           alpha = frac * a_mean / hmax
           cand = a - alpha * h
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
           sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
           if sc_c < local_sc - 1e-15:
               local_sc=sc_c; local_a=cand.copy()
       if local_sc < sc - 1e-15:
           a = _normalize(local_a); fail=0
       else:
           fail += 1
           if fail > 8:
               a = _normalize(local? a? )
               if fail > 12:
                  a = _normalize(0.8*best_a + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n))
               else:
                  a = _normalize(a + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n))
               fail=0
       if it % 10 ==0:
           K = min(K_end, K*1.05 +0.05)
       it +=1
       if it > 5000: break
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Multiplicative factor: if h positive (contributes to high bins), exp(-eta h) reduces; if h negative (below average), h negative => exp(+), increase. Good.
Euclidean `a - alpha*h` reduces high, increases low below average.

Potential issue: `cand = a * np.exp(-eta*h)` may under/overflow if eta*h large. `frac/hmax` times h; max exponent = frac, between 0.001 and 0.2? Wait if hmax max, eta*hmax=frac. So exponent within [ -0.2? If h positive max -> exp(-frac) >=0.8; if h negative min -> h/hmax maybe -dmin/hmax <=1, exp(+frac)<=1.22. Very mild. We wanted up to 0.2 relative. Good. Maybe need more aggressive: fracs up to 1.0, 3.0? Since h/hmax <=1, exponent max = frac. If frac=1.0, relative change e^{-1}=0.37 max. Could allow 1.0. Use fracs (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0) but h distribution maybe hmax much larger than typical; to move low cells, negative h maybe small? If min h ≈ -hmax, then increase factor e^{frac}; up to e^1=2.7, okay. Use fracs (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,0.7,1.5). Euclidean fracs up to 1.0? `alpha=hmax?` Shift by up to a_mean (if frac=1), too large. Use up to 0.3. Good.

Let's set multiplicative fracs: (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0). Exponents up to 2, e^2=7 for very negative h, may be aggressive. But line search picks best; okay. Euclidean fracs: (0.001,0.005,0.02,0.1,0.3,0.8). Good.

`_optimize_lbfgs`:

def _optimize_lbfgs(a, beta_factor, maxiter=60):
    if _scipy_minimize is None: return a,_score_fft(a)
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n<2: return a,_score_fft(a)
    c=_fft_self(a); maxc=float(np.max(c)); beta=max(1.0,beta_factor/max(maxc,1e-12))
    _LBFGS_STATE['beta']=beta
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=_center_clip(z,70)
    x0=z.copy()
    bounds=[(-70.0,70.0)]*n
    try:
       res = _scipy_minimize(_lbfgs_fun, x0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':20,'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       res=None
    z2 = res.x if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') else x0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z2)): z2=x0
    a2 = _softmax(z2); a2=_normalize(a2)
    sc2=_score_fft(a2)
    sc1=_score_fft(a)
    if sc2 < sc1: return a2, sc2
    return a, sc1
```
`_lbfgs_fun` as above. Ensure it centers x and uses `_LBFGS_STATE['beta']`.

`_optimize_stage`:
```
def _optimize_stage(a, deadline, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return a,_score_fft(a)
    total = max(1.0, float(deadline - t0))
    used_frac = 0.0
    if allow_lsq and n <= 512 and _scipy_minimize is not None and total > 30:
       frac = 0.35 if n <= 256 else 0.22
       end = min(deadline - 25, t0 + total*frac)
       if end > time.monotonic() + 5:
          a, s = _optimize_ls(a, end, rng)
       used_frac = frac
    # natural
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       if n <= 256: frac = 0.35
       elif n <= 512: frac = 0.35
       elif n <= 1024: frac = 0.5
       else: frac = 0.6
       end = min(deadline - 15, time.monotonic() + max(10.0, total*frac))
       a, s = _optimize_natural(a, end, rng)
    # L-BFGS
    if allow_lbfgs and n <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       if n <= 256: maxiter = 70
       elif n <= 512: maxiter = 40
       else: maxiter = 25
       a2, s2 = _optimize_lbfgs(a, 25.0, maxiter=maxiter)
       a, s = a2, s2
    # subgrad polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       a, s = _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng)
    return a, s
```
This doesn't strictly allocate fractions but okay. If LSQ uses less, natural gets frac based on total, could exceed deadline? It is capped by deadline. If LSQ uses more, natural start later but `total*frac` from t0, may have already used; `time()+...` still after LSQ maybe less. Good.

Could L-BFGS consume too much time and leave no subgrad. It has no time cap. Use smaller maxiter. For n=256 70 iterations maybe 5s; for 512 40 maybe 10s; okay. If time before 20s and maxiter maybe 70; if actual 20s, subgrad little. We can refine: only call if `deadline - now > 30` and estimated time < 0.5*(remaining). But hard. Use if remaining >60 for n<=256, >90 for 512, >120 for 1024. Let's set:
```
remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
if n<=256 and remaining>60: L-BFGS 70
elif n<=512 and remaining>100: 40
elif n<=1024 and remaining>150: 25
```
Then L-BFGS won't squeeze too much. Good.

```
def _select_best_inputs(inits, max_count=3):
    scored=[]
    for i,a in enumerate(inits):
        a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
        if a.size==0: continue
        a=_normalize(a)
        sc=_score_fft(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
        sym = _normalize(0.5*(a + a[::-1]))
        scs = _score_fft(sym)
        if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < sc:
            a=sym; sc=scs
        scored.append((float(sc), i, a))
    scored.sort()
    out=[]
    for sc,i,a in scored:
       if len(out)>=max_count: break
       # avoid exact duplicate? skip
       out.append(a)
    return out
```

def _optimize_ls(a, deadline, rng, max_lsq_n=512, K_start=4.0, K_end=40.0):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fft(a)
   if n==0 or n>max_lsq_n or _scipy_minimize is None: return best_a,best_sc
   if rng is None: rng=default
   L=2*n-1; uniformW=np.full(L,1/L)
   K=K_start; mix=0.4; fail=0; it=0; last=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      c=_fft_self(a); maxc=max(c); sc=2*n*maxc
      if sc < best_sc: best...
      beta=K/max(maxc,1e-12)
      Wc,_=_lse_w_val(c,beta)
      W=(1-mix)*uniformW + mix*Wc
      W += 1e-5 # avoid zeros? W=(W+1e-4*uniformW); normalize
      W=W/W.sum()
      # expected QP time? remaining = deadline-now; maxiter = ...
      rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if n <= 256:
          maxit = int(min(60, max(6, rem*4)))  # if rem 100 ->60? 400 cap 60
      else:
          maxit = int(min(30, max(6, rem*2)))
      # if maxit<4 break
      maxit = int(max(maxit,4))
      newa = _solve_qp_target(a,W,maxit)
      if newa is None: newa=a
      # line search
      local_a=a; local_sc=sc
      for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          cand=(1.0-t)*a + t*newa
          cand=_normalize(cand)
          sc_c=_score_fft(cand)
          if sc_c < local_sc:
             local_sc=sc_c; local_a=cand.copy()
      if local_sc < sc - 1e-12:
          a=local_a; fail=0
      else:
          fail +=1
          # try a small noisy mix if candidate near
          cand=_normalize(0.85*a + 0.15*newa)
          sc_c=_score_fft(cand)
          if sc_c <= sc + 1e-7:
             a=cand; fail=0
          else:
             a=_normalize(a + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n))
             fail=0
      K = min(K_end, K*1.03 +0.05)
      mix = min(1.0, mix +0.02)
      if it - last > 15:
          K=max(K_start*K*0.5? just max(K_start,K*0.6)); mix=0.4
          fail=0
      last update if local improve? Do if sc < best before? We'll track last when best_sc improved.
      if sc < best_sc: last=it
      it +=1
      if it > 10000: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Now, possible bug in `_lbfgs_fun`: We center z each call but gradient computed for centered z. L-BFGS uses x derivatives; since we map x -> z = x-mean, the Jacobian should include centering: derivative of z wrt x = I - (1/n)11ᵀ. Our returned gradient `grad` is derivative wrt z, not x. Need account! Important. In L-BFGS, objective function should be f(x) = F(z(x)) where z=x-mean(x). Gradient wrt x = (I - P) grad_z. Because mean shift. We also added penalty 0.5*pen*z·z; gradient wrt x includes (I-P)pen*z. Our `_lbfgs_fun` returns `grad + pen*z` (with no projection) after centering. Actually if z=x-mean, dz/dx = I - 11ᵀ/n. Thus grad_x = (I-P)grad_z. My code `grad -= mean(grad)` (projection) but that's not exactly (I-P)grad_z? (I-P)grad = grad - mean(grad) (since 1ᵀz/n times 1? For any vector g, (I-P)g = g - mean(g)1. Yes. So gradient wrt x = grad - mean(grad). We did `grad -= mean(grad)`. Good. For penalty, z depends on x; penalty gradient wrt x = pen*(I-P)z. But z = x - mean(x); (I-P)z = z (since z already centered). So add pen*z. Good. So correct.
But note `_lbfgs_fun` adds pen=1e-8*0.5*dot(z,z); grad includes 1e-8*z. Good.

In L-BFGS final, if x has mean not zero, fun centers. Good.

In natural, we use z directly centered. Fine.

```python
"""
Low-peak nonnegative self-convolution search.

This program searches for a nonnegative sequence a of length n minimizing

    score(a) = 2 n max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2

where * denotes linear convolution.  The sequence is therefore optimized mainly
in its shape; scale is irrelevant.

The algorithm combines several different mechanisms:

1. Diverse initializations:
   - previous best sequence if available,
   - parametric U-shaped / cosine blends,
   - spectral-factor style inits from the square root of the flat-window DFT.

2. Alternating KKT-style equalization:
   - select weights W on convolution bins (mostly high bins),
   - solve a convex QP with SLSQP that equalizes weighted pair-correlation
     values Ma ~ constant on the simplex.

3. Natural-gradient / LSE optimization in softmax parameters.

4. L-BFGS-B polishing of the smooth log-sum-exp surrogate.

5. Hard active-bin subgradient projected line search.

The search is staged by length (256, 512, 1024, 2048/4096) and the best exact
score candidate found among these lengths is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore", divide="ignore")

_QP_STATE = {"M": None, "n": 0}
_LBFGS_STATE = {"beta": 1.0}


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _next_pow2(size):
    size = int(max(1, size))
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N).real
    b = b[:L]
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_fft(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _exact_score_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    if n <= 8192:
        b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        b = _fft_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s)


def _lse_w_val(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64), m
    arg = beta * (c - m)
    arg = np.clip(arg, -745.0, 709.0)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64), m
    w = e / s
    val = m + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    return w, val


def _corr(W, a):
    W = np.asarray(W, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if W.size != 2 * n - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        L = min(tmp.size, W.size)
        if L > 0:
            tmp[:L] = W[:L]
        W = tmp
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(W, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Ar * Wr, N).real
    H = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    H = np.where(H > 0.0, H, 0.0)
    return H


def _center_clip(z, L=70.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=float(L), neginf=-float(L))
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -float(L), float(L))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.where(z < -745.0, -745.0, z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_smooth_random(n, rng, base, amplitude=0.5):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if base.size != n:
        base = _make_uniform(n)
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cutoff = max(2, min(len(F), n // 12 + 2))
    if cutoff < len(F):
        F[cutoff:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n).real
    f = f - float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 0.0:
        f = f / sd
    a = base * (1.0 + float(amplitude) * f)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    return _normalize(a)


def _param_inits(n, rng, count=24):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    out = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)

    qs = (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0)
    amps = (0.3, 0.6, 0.9)
    ms = (1, 2, 3, 5)
    k = 0
    for q in qs:
        for amp in amps:
            if k >= count:
                break
            m = ms[k % len(ms)]
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a = 0.12 + 0.88 * (d ** q)
            a = a + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
            out.append(_normalize(a))
            k += 1
        if k >= count:
            break

    for q in (0.9, 1.8, 2.8):
        a = 0.1 + 0.9 * (d ** q)
        out.append(_normalize(a))

    for _ in range(3):
        out.append(_make_smooth_random(n, rng, _make_uniform(n), 0.6))

    return out


def _spectral_init(n, rng, iters=6):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    minN = max(4 * n, L + 1)
    while N < minN:
        N <<= 1

    target = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    target[:L] = 1.0 / float(L)
    T = np.fft.fft(target)
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(T)))
    eps = 1e-10 * max(1.0, amax)
    des = np.sqrt(T + eps)

    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    variants = (des.copy(), -des.copy())
    for A0 in variants:
        A = A0.copy()

        a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            a = a / s
            sc = _score_fft(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()

        for _ in range(int(max(0, iters))):
            a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                a = a / s
                sc = _score_fft(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()
            else:
                a = _normalize(0.1 + 0.9 * np.abs(2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n) - 1.0))

            Ap = np.fft.fft(a, N)
            ratio = des / (Ap + 1e-12 + 0.0j)
            mag = np.abs(ratio)
            mag = np.clip(mag, 0.2, 5.0)
            ratio = mag * np.exp(1j * np.angle(ratio))
            A = Ap * (0.7 + 0.3 * ratio)
            A[0] = float(np.abs(A[0]))

            a = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                a = a / s
                sc = _score_fft(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()

    if best is None:
        best = _normalize(0.1 + 0.9 * np.abs(2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n) - 1.0))
    return _normalize(best)


def _resample_sequence(old, new_n):
    old = np.asarray(old, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old = np.nan_to_num(old, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    old = np.maximum(old, 0.0)
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    if old.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(new_n)
    s = float(old.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return _make_uniform(new_n)
    old = old / s
    if old.size == new_n:
        return old.copy()
    if old.size == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / float(new_n), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return _normalize(new)


def _get_given_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _select_best_inputs(inits, max_count=3):
    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(inits):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        a = _normalize(a)
        sc = _score_fft(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        sym = _normalize(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
        scs = _score_fft(sym)
        if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < sc:
            a = sym
            sc = scs
        scored.append((float(sc), i, a))
    scored.sort()
    out = []
    for sc, i, a in scored:
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
        out.append(a)
    return out


def _record_candidate(a, sc, by_len):
    if a is None:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or n > 8192:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if n not in by_len or sc < by_len[n][1] - 1e-12:
        by_len[n] = (a.copy(), float(sc))


def _qp_eq(x):
    n = _QP_STATE["n"]
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 1.0)


def _qp_eq_jac(x):
    n = _QP_STATE["n"]
    jac = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    jac[:n] = 1.0
    jac[n] = 0.0
    return jac


def _qp_fun(x):
    M = _QP_STATE["M"]
    n = _QP_STATE["n"]
    if M is None or n <= 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = x[:n]
    lam = float(x[n])
    r = M.dot(a) - lam
    f = 0.5 * float(np.dot(r, r))
    g = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    g[:n] = M.dot(r)
    g[n] = -float(np.sum(r))
    return f, g


def _solve_qp_target(a, W, maxiter):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    L = 2 * n - 1
    rows = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
    cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
    idx = rows + cols
    M = float(L) * W[idx]
    del idx
    del rows
    del cols

    _QP_STATE["M"] = M
    _QP_STATE["n"] = n
    a = _normalize(a)
    lam0 = float(np.mean(M.dot(a)))
    x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    x0[:n] = a
    x0[n] = lam0

    lam_bound = max(10.0, 10.0 * float(L))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(-lam_bound, lam_bound)]
    cons = {"type": "eq", "fun": _qp_eq, "jac": _qp_eq_jac}

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _qp_fun,
            x0,
            jac=True,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(max(4, maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
        )
    except Exception:
        _QP_STATE["M"] = None
        return a

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        _QP_STATE["M"] = None
        return a

    na = np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    na = np.maximum(na, 0.0)
    s = float(na.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        _QP_STATE["M"] = None
        return a
    na = na / s
    _QP_STATE["M"] = None
    return na


def _optimize_ls(a, deadline, rng=None, max_lsq_n=512, K_start=4.0, K_end=40.0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or n > max_lsq_n or _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a, _score_fft(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    uniformW = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    K = float(K_start)
    mix = 0.40
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_self(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            last = it

        beta = K / max(maxc, 1e-12)
        Wc, _ = _lse_w_val(c, beta)

        use_uniform = rng.random() < 0.18
        if use_uniform:
            W = uniformW.copy()
        else:
            W = (1.0 - mix) * uniformW + mix * Wc
            W = W + 1e-5 * uniformW
            ws = float(W.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                W = uniformW.copy()
            else:
                W = W / ws

        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if n <= 256:
            maxit = int(min(60, max(6, rem * 4.0)))
        else:
            maxit = int(min(30, max(6, rem * 2.0)))
        maxit = max(4, maxit)

        newa = _solve_qp_target(a, W, maxit)
        if newa is None:
            newa = a

        local_a = a
        local_sc = sc
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * newa
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < local_sc - 1e-15:
                local_sc = sc_c
                local_a = cand.copy()

        if local_sc < sc - 1e-14:
            a = local_a
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            cand = _normalize(0.85 * a + 0.15 * newa)
            sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c <= sc + 1e-7:
                a = cand
            else:
                a = _normalize(a + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            fail = 0

        K = min(float(K_end), K * 1.03 + 0.05)
        mix = min(1.0, mix + 0.02)

        if it - last > 15:
            K = max(float(K_start), K * 0.6)
            mix = 0.40
            fail = 0

        it += 1
        if it > 12000:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _optimize_natural(a, deadline, rng=None, beta_factor_start=8.0, beta_factor_end=150.0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a, _score_fft(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)

    Lb = 70.0 if n <= 2048 else 60.0
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = _center_clip(z, Lb)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(a)
    best_z = z.copy()

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.15
    K = float(beta_factor_start)
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_self(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_z = _center_clip(np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)), Lb)
            last = it

        beta = K / max(maxc, 1e-12)
        W, val = _lse_w_val(c, beta)
        H = _corr(W, a)
        fbar = float(np.dot(W, c))
        grad = 2.0 * a * (H - fbar)
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            z = best_z + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
            a = _softmax(z)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.15
            K = max(float(beta_factor_start), K * 0.5)
            fail = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if maxg <= 1e-16:
            z = best_z + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
            a = _softmax(z)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.15
            fail = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        t += 1
        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * grad
        v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - 0.9 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t)
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)

        val_old = val
        max_old = maxc
        accepted = False
        step_try = step

        for _ls in range(10):
            zt = z - step_try
            zt = _center_clip(zt, Lb)
            pt = _softmax(zt)
            cpt = _fft_self(pt)
            mt = float(np.max(cpt))
            _, vtp = _lse_w_val(cpt, beta)

            if np.isfinite(mt) and mt < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = mt
                best_a = pt.copy()
                best_z = zt.copy()
                last = it

            if (np.isfinite(vtp) and vtp < val_old - 1e-13) or (np.isfinite(mt) and mt < max_old - 1e-13):
                z = zt
                a = pt
                val = vtp
                maxc = mt
                accepted = True
                break

            step_try = step_try * 0.5
            if float(np.max(np.abs(step_try))) < 1e-9:
                break

        if accepted:
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < val_old - 1e-13:
                lr = min(0.5, lr * 1.1)
            else:
                fail += 1
        else:
            fail += 4
            lr *= 0.6
            if fail > 80:
                z = best_z + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
                a = _softmax(z)
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                lr = 0.15
                K = max(float(beta_factor_start), K * 0.5)
                fail = 0

        if it - last > 250 and it > 50:
            z = best_z + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = _center_clip(z, Lb)
            a = _softmax(z)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.15
            K = max(float(beta_factor_start), K * 0.7)
            fail = 0

        if it % 8 == 0:
            K = min(float(beta_factor_end), K * 1.04 + 0.02)

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng=None, K_start=8.0, K_end=60.0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a, _score_fft(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(24680)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(a)
    K = float(K_start)
    fail = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_self(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        beta = K / max(maxc, 1e-12)
        W, _ = _lse_w_val(c, beta)
        H = _corr(W, a)
        h = H - float(np.mean(H))
        hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))

        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-300:
            a = _normalize(a + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            fail += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        a_mean = float(np.mean(a))
        local_a = a
        local_sc = sc

        for frac in (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            eta = frac / hmax
            cand = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < local_sc - 1e-15:
                local_sc = sc_c
                local_a = cand.copy()

        for frac in (0.001, 0.005, 0.02, 0.1, 0.3, 0.8):
            alpha = frac * a_mean / hmax
            cand = a - alpha * h
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            sc_c = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < local_sc - 1e-15:
                local_sc = sc_c
                local_a = cand.copy()

        if local_sc < sc - 1e-14:
            a = _normalize(local_a)
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 12:
                a = _normalize(0.8 * best_a + 0.2 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            else:
                a = _normalize(a + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            fail = 0

        if it % 10 == 0:
            K = min(float(K_end), K * 1.05 + 0.05)

        it += 1
        if it > 6000:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _lbfgs_fun(x):
    beta = float(_LBFGS_STATE["beta"])
    z = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    p = _softmax(z)
    c = _fft_self(p)
    W, val = _lse_w_val(c, beta)
    H = _corr(W, p)
    fbar = float(np.dot(W, c))

    grad = 2.0 * p * (H - fbar)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))

    pen = 0.5 * 1e-8 * float(np.dot(z, z))
    g = grad + 1e-8 * z
    return val + pen, g


def _optimize_lbfgs(a, beta_factor, maxiter=60):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a, _score_fft(a)
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, _score_fft(a)

    c = _fft_self(a)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_factor) / max(maxc, 1e-12))
    _LBFGS_STATE["beta"] = beta

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = _center_clip(z, 70.0)
    x0 = z.copy()
    bounds = [(-70.0, 70.0)] * n

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _lbfgs_fun,
            x0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(max(5, maxiter)),
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    z2 = getattr(res, "x", x0) if res is not None else x0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z2)):
        z2 = x0

    a2 = _softmax(z2)
    a2 = _normalize(a2)
    sc2 = _score_fft(a2)
    sc1 = _score_fft(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc1:
        return a2, sc2
    return a, sc1


def _optimize_stage(a, deadline, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a, _score_fft(a)

    total = max(1.0, float(deadline - t0))

    if allow_lsq and n <= 512 and _scipy_minimize is not None and total > 30.0:
        frac = 0.35 if n <= 256 else 0.22
        end = min(deadline - 25.0, t0 + total * frac)
        if end > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
            a, _ = _optimize_ls(a, end, rng)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        if n <= 256:
            frac = 0.35
        elif n <= 512:
            frac = 0.38
        elif n <= 1024:
            frac = 0.50
        else:
            frac = 0.60
        end = min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, total * frac))
        a, _ = _optimize_natural(a, end, rng)

    remaining = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if allow_lbfgs and n <= 1024:
        if n <= 256 and remaining > 60.0:
            a2, s2 = _optimize_lbfgs(a, 25.0, maxiter=70)
            a, s = a2, s2
        elif n <= 512 and remaining > 100.0:
            a2, s2 = _optimize_lbfgs(a, 25.0, maxiter=40)
            a, s = a2, s2
        elif n <= 1024 and remaining > 150.0:
            a2, s2 = _optimize_lbfgs(a, 25.0, maxiter=25)
            a, s = a2, s2

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        a, _ = _subgrad_polish(a, deadline, rng)

    return a, _score_fft(a)


def _update_best_from_result(a_opt, s_opt, by_len):
    if a_opt is None:
        return None, np.inf
    a_opt = _normalize(a_opt)
    s_opt = _score_fft(a_opt)
    if not np.isfinite(s_opt):
        return None, np.inf
    sym = _normalize(0.5 * (a_opt + a_opt[::-1]))
    s_sym = _score_fft(sym)
    if np.isfinite(s_sym) and s_sym < s_opt:
        a_opt = sym
        s_opt = s_sym
    _record_candidate(a_opt, s_opt, by_len)
    return a_opt, float(s_opt)


def _final_eval_candidate(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    sc = _exact_score_seq(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        sym = _normalize(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
        scs = _exact_score_seq(sym)
        if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < sc:
            return sym, scs
    return a, sc


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point.  Returns a nonnegative list with low score."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    reserve = min(30.0, max(2.0, budget * 0.015))
    if budget > reserve + 30.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(budget - reserve, 1.0)
    else:
        deadline = t0 + max(0.9 * budget, 1.0)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    by_len = {}
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    given = _get_given_sequence()

    if given is not None and given.size > 0 and given.size <= 8192:
        gsc = _score_fft(given)
        if np.isfinite(gsc):
            best_a = given.copy()
            best_sc = float(gsc)
            _record_candidate(given, gsc, by_len)

    if best_a is None or best_a.size > 8192:
        u = _make_uniform(256)
        us = _score_fft(u)
        if us < best_sc or best_a is None:
            best_a = u
            best_sc = us
        _record_candidate(u, us, by_len)

    print(f"initial best len={best_a.size} score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    available = max(1.0, budget - reserve)
    scale = min(1.0, max(0.0, available / 2400.0))
    if scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 0.0

    # ---------------- length 256 ----------------
    n0 = 256
    inits = []
    if given is not None:
        inits.append(_resample_sequence(given, n0))
    inits.extend(_param_inits(n0, rng, count=22))
    inits.append(_spectral_init(n0, rng, iters=5))
    seeds0 = _select_best_inputs(inits, 3)
    if not seeds0:
        seeds0 = [best_a.copy()]

    for a in seeds0:
        a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(a, None, by_len)
        if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = s2

    base256 = 820.0 * scale
    end256 = min(deadline, t0 + base256)
    if time.monotonic() < end256 - 30.0 and len(seeds0) >= 1:
        for idx, a0 in enumerate(seeds0):
            remaining_seeds = max(1, len(seeds0) - idx)
            share = max(30.0, (end256 - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_seeds))
            sub_end = min(end256, time.monotonic() + share)
            if time.monotonic() < sub_end - 10.0:
                try:
                    ao, so = _optimize_stage(a0, sub_end, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True)
                    a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                    if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        best_sc = s2
                    print(f"256 restart {idx+1}/{len(seeds0)} score={s2:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if time.monotonic() < end256 - 20.0 and 256 in by_len:
        try:
            cur = np.asarray(by_len[256][0], dtype=np.float64)
            ao, so = _optimize_stage(cur, end256, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True)
            a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
            if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_a = a2.copy()
                best_sc = s2
            print(f"256 polish best score={s2:.10f} global={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ---------------- length 512 ----------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        n1 = 512
        base1 = 560.0 * scale
        end1 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + base1)
        if time.monotonic() < end1 - 60.0:
            inits = []
            if best_a is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(best_a, n1))
            if given is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(given, n1))
            inits.extend(_param_inits(n1, rng, count=10))
            inits.append(_spectral_init(n1, rng, iters=4))
            seeds1 = _select_best_inputs(inits, 2)
            if seeds1:
                for idx, a0 in enumerate(seeds1):
                    remaining_seeds = max(1, len(seeds1) - idx)
                    share = max(40.0, (end1 - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_seeds))
                    sub_end = min(end1, time.monotonic() + share)
                    if time.monotonic() < sub_end - 15.0:
                        try:
                            ao, so = _optimize_stage(a0, sub_end, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True)
                            a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                            if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                                best_a = a2.copy()
                                best_sc = s2
                            print(f"512 restart {idx+1}/{len(seeds1)} score={s2:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

            if time.monotonic() < end1 - 20.0 and (512 in by_len or best_a.size == 512):
                cur = np.asarray(by_len.get(512, (best_a, None))[0], dtype=np.float64)
                try:
                    ao, so = _optimize_stage(cur, end1, rng, allow_lsq=True, allow_lbfgs=True)
                    a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                    if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        best_sc = s2
                    print(f"512 polish score={s2:.10f} global={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # ---------------- length 1024 ----------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        n2 = 1024
        base2 = 460.0 * scale
        end2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + base2)
        if time.monotonic() < end2 - 60.0:
            inits = []
            if best_a is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(best_a, n2))
            if given is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(given, n2))
            if 512 in by_len:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(by_len[512][0], n2))
            inits.extend(_param_inits(n2, rng, count=6))
            inits.append(_spectral_init(n2, rng, iters=3))
            seeds2 = _select_best_inputs(inits, 2)
            if seeds2:
                for idx, a0 in enumerate(seeds2):
                    remaining_seeds = max(1, len(seeds2) - idx)
                    share = max(5.0, (end2 - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining_seeds))
                    sub_end = min(end2, time.monotonic() + share)
                    if time.monotonic() < sub_end - 10.0:
                        try:
                            ao, so = _optimize_stage(a0, sub_end, rng, allow_lsq=False, allow_lbfgs=True)
                            a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                            if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                                best_a = a2.copy()
                                best_sc = s2
                            print(f"1024 restart {idx+1}/{len(seeds2)} score={s2:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

                if time.monotonic() < end2 - 15.0 and (1024 in by_len):
                    cur = np.asarray(by_len[1024][0], dtype=np.float64)
                    try:
                        ao, so = _optimize_stage(cur, end2, rng, allow_lsq=False, allow_lbfgs=True)
                        a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                        if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            best_a = a2.copy()
                            best_sc = s2
                        print(f"1024 polish score={s2:.10f} global={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    # ---------------- length 2048 / 4096 ----------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        if best_a is not None and best_a.size >= 2048:
            n3 = 4096
        else:
            n3 = 2048
        base3 = 340.0 * scale
        end3 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + base3)
        if time.monotonic() < end3 - 50.0:
            inits = []
            if best_a is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(best_a, n3))
            if given is not None:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(given, n3))
            if 1024 in by_len:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(by_len[1024][0], n3))
            if n3 == 2048 and 512 in by_len:
                inits.append(_resample_sequence(by_len[512][0], n3))
            inits.extend(_param_inits(n3, rng, count=4))
            seeds3 = _select_best_inputs(inits, 1)
            if seeds3:
                try:
                    ao, so = _optimize_stage(seeds3[0], end3, rng, allow_lsq=False, allow_lbfgs=(n3 <= 2048))
                    a2, s2 = _update_best_from_result(ao, so, by_len)
                    if a2 is not None and s2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        best_sc = s2
                    print(f"{n3} long score={s2:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # ---------------- final exact selection ----------------
    final_candidates = []
    for arr, sc in by_len.values():
        final_candidates.append(arr)
    if best_a is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_a)

    final_a = None
    final_sc = np.inf
    for cand in final_candidates:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        a_f, s_f = _final_eval_candidate(cand)
        if np.isfinite(s_f) and s_f < final_sc:
            final_a = a_f
            final_sc = s_f

    if final_a is None:
        final_a = _make_uniform(256)
        final_sc = _exact_score_seq(final_a)

    if final_a.size > 8192:
        final_a = _resample_sequence(final_a, 4096)
        final_a, final_sc = _final_eval_candidate(final_a)

    out = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        out = _make_uniform(256)
        s = 1.0
    out = out / s
    seq = [float(x) for x in out]

    # Safety check against the official evaluator if it is present.
    evaluator = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if evaluator is not None:
        try:
            ev = evaluator(seq)
            if not np.isfinite(ev):
                seq = [float(x) for x in _make_uniform(256)]
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"final length={len(seq)} exact_score={final_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
    return seq
```
